# Overframe Community Builds — Warframes
Community-curated build snapshots scraped from Overframe.gg. These are popular Tenno-submitted loadouts, NOT official game data. Every entry is flagged `(community-curated, source: Overframe)` and must never override authoritative stats from equipment/weapons/mods/export bundles. Each item carries up to three roles: `top` (highest score — meta/endgame pick), `utility` (title-matched support / CC / farming / stealth / subsume build), and `runner-up` (second highest score). Prefer `top` or `runner-up` for endgame queries; use `utility` only when the request is for support / CC / farming / Helminth context. Snapshot fetched 2026-05-15T19:58:48.835Z; up to 3 per item; enriched=yes.

### Author Guide
Can use fewer Forma if you have an Umbral Forma. Here's the gist: [Ore Gaze] guarantees loot dropped at >=400% strength (with no upper limit). [Rubble Heap] lets you cast Landslide for free when you're at 1400 rubble collected. [Path of Statues] applies Petrify to enemies hit by Landslide. We don't care too much about efficiency. You'll be casting Landslide for free and the only other ability you'll be casting is Petrify. But you'll be picking up Energy Orbs regularly enough to keep up the strength bonus from [Energy Conversion]. And use a status priming weapon to keep up the bonus from [Growing Power]. Duration definitely could use a bit of love so feel free to swap out [Arcane Energize] for [Molt Efficiency] if you want (the bonus to duration doesn't display correctly here). Or, if yo
...

For even more crowd control, Rest makes enemies fall asleep, as well as forcing stealth melee bonus (700% damage additive to crit multiplier). Now that Garuda's passive has changed from being based on missing health to the number of kills, there is now no reason to run a traditional 2hp/quick thinking/tank build. This means we lose access to armor stripping so ensure you bring either some way to deal with armor or damage that goes straight through it by abusing slash procs. ### Author Guide
How Nourish (2) works. Casting Nourish (2) on different units gives you different buffs, they are as followed; - Heavy, flying or summoned units: Strike buff (Adds toxin damage to all attacks for nearby friendlies) - Melee units: Armor Buff (Deal toxin damage around friendlies whenever they take damage) - Ranged units: Energy Buff (Multiplies the energy gained for all nearby friendlies) The buff Nourish (2) you obtain is determined by which enemy was consumed first by feast, if multiple enemies were consumed by feast you can cycle through buffs by holding Nourish (2) down. If you wish to have an additional 10% strength feel free to install a maxed Transient Fortitude (rank 10). I chose a Rank 8 to have the buff as close to 1 minute duration possible. You can easily hold 3 enemies

### Author Guide
A Hydroid build for those who don't want to use orokin reactors or forma. Get yourselves some corrupted mods and Pilfering Swarm and you got yourself an efficient farmer. Even if he has low duration the tentacles stay for a while after the timer ends, but do still spam it every 6-7 seconds (this is why efficiency is important). Energy Siphon and Zenurik school is not needed but highly recommended since it could stop you from ever running out of energy, for max farming take a Nekros with a desecrate build with you. If you prefer to kill them yourselves and only use the tentacles for extra loot, you could switch out power strength for either more duration or survivability.

My Zariman Limbo Farming. Press up on your D-pad to get the bigger map on your screen, go near a cluster of canisters, and then use your 4th ability Cataclysm two times in quick succession to break all the canisters in the area. If something doesn't break/move while using your 4th and won't get picked up by your companion's Fetch etc, then that's a Voidplume or some other treasures that you're looking for. This build is simple, with max range, as much Efficiency as possible, and some smaller duration to keep your Infested mobility alive long enough for you to regain the energy spent on it while in the void. ## INTRODUCTION This is a DPS Limbo build focused on buffing weapon damage with the Rift Torrent augment and combining this with Breach Surge to create 'double dipped' surge sparks which can do damage in the billions or even negative billions (due to integer overflow). This is possible because Breach Surge spark damage scale off your weapon damage which is multiplied by the Rift Torrent bonus, but each spark itself is also counted as a weapon, so it is multiplied by the Rift Torrent bonus a second time when it hits. In addition, Limbo's stasis and cataclysm abilities allow you to get easy headshots in the absolute safety of the rift. ## Infinite Invis (0 Forma, No Primed Mods) — Loki (community-curated, source: Overframe)
Role: utility | Author: Touchstone | Votes: 38 | Forma: 0 | MR: 0 | Overframe build #30465

### Mod Loadout
- S1 (empty)
- S2 Vitality
- S3 Streamline
- S4 Continuity
- S5 Constitution
- S6 Augur Message
- S7 Fleeting Expertise
- S8 Narrow Minded
- S9 Energy Siphon
- S10 Thief's Wit

### Author Guide
## The Case for Fleeting Expertise Maximizing Ability Efficiency at the expense of Ability Duration is more effective than not. With it on this build, Invisibility lasts for 26.52 seconds and costs 12.5 Energy per cast. While under the effects of Invisibility you will regenerate 15.912 Energy thanks to Energy Siphon, functionally allowing you to continue casting Invisibility for as long as you'd like. On the other hand, without it Invisibility lasts for 33.72 seconds and costs 35 Energy per cast. While under its effects you will regain 20.232 Energy. Unlike the build with it, this means that you run an Energy deficit every time you cast. So go forward with your infinite invisibility. Scan Kavats, I guess?

Stretch is just taking up a slot so it can replaced with something like Enemy Sense, Natural Talent or really anything you want if you wish. (Example Flow being half its normal cost, and Steel Charge being double) if a mod is of a different polarity it takes more capacity. Aura: This Mod Slot is the only slot that is good to put a mod in regardless of the mod itself, It increases your capacity by the mods displayed amount in the top right corner. Good Choices from Nightwave: Steel Charge (18 extra capacity), pistol, rifle, Shotgun Amp (14 capacity) (Only 5 Extra Capacity equip Energy Siphon or Corrosive Projection) Order of mods to put on 1. ### Author Guide
Hi! I'm wynnie, my ign is "bnuy", and I'm a Mag one-trick and have over 1,000,000 kills with her. She's my favourite frame, I adore her fashion, her theme, and her play-style. ## INTRODUCTION Magnetize is Mag's most versatile and powerful ability and can be used to crowd control/group enemies, block enemy projectiles, shred a single target (bosses/acolytes) or grouped enemies, AOE nuke (line of sight) and finally disarm enemies (with the augment). Magnetize works by creating a bubble around an enemy and redirecting all projectiles/bullets into the centre. This will allow weapons with punch through to rapidly kill enemies in the centre by hitting them multiple times as they orbit around. Additionally, 25% of your weapon's damage will be absorbed into the bubble (counts multishot but not crits or DoT effects). Safeguard is a slightly more controversial choice, as it provides no benefit to Nezha. However, granting allies 90% damage reduction + immunity to status effects is arguably one of the best buffs in the game and can really help frames which struggle with survivability. Hello everyone, I'm a Nezha main for some years now and thought I could share my builds aswell. Danse deals damage equal to enemy damage absorbed, but this decays rapidly over time unless you continue to take damage. Danse Macabre also has innate armor strip since it procs corrosive status on any armored enemies. By levelcap, enemies typically deal up to 1 million damage per shot, meaning each revolution of nine lasers deals 9 million damage after just one shot. Dark Propagation allows enemies killed to self spread Sow, meaning it can spread across a wider area without recasting Sow. Enemies affected by Sow who are then subsequently hit by Reap take true damage equal to 25% of their CURRENT health. This value is NOT affected by ability strength, but is affected by Viral (2x at 1 stack and 4.25x at 10 stacks) and Roar. You need exactly 255% strength on both Reap and Roar to one-shot enemies without viral status. ## INTRODUCTION This build focuses on maximising weapon DPS through 100% armor strip from Abating Link and two sources of critical chance from Champion's Blessing and Arcane Avenger. Champion's Blessing provides up to a maximum of 350% additive crit chance based on % health healed, while Arcane Avenger provides a flat 45% boost to final crit chance. This means if you use the Kuva Bramma with Point Strike alone, you hit a final crit chance of 255%, or every second hit is a red crit. You can optionally use a Bleeding Dragon Key to drop your max health which means the % healed is increased per Bless cast and the crit chance boost is sped up, however, you will want to use Quick Thinking to prevent accidental death. Without a bleeding dragon key it will take approximately 2 to 5 mins to ramp

**VYTHELAS BUFF** Vythelas's heat buff is identical to Cyte's resupply (and a cousin of Saryn/Oraxia). Note that HEAT MODS are mandatory as they double dip the heat DoT and FACTION MODS are ideal too as they TRIPLE DIP the heat DoT. Vythelas Heat DoT = 0.5 x 30% x stregnth x base damage x (1 + HEAT MOD + other elements) x (1+ HEAT MOD) x (1+FACTION)^3 # HELMINTH OPTIONS **THERMAL SUNDER** Thermal Sunder with Thermal Transfer augment gives +75% heat x strength buff on casting heat sunder, which feeds into Vythelas heat buff twice. **NOURISH** Nourish boosts raw damage by +45% viral x strength, that still affects Vythelas heat DoT while viral status boosts all damage by up to 4.25x, and allows you to drop viral mods on weapon. **ROAR** You can subsume Roar over 3 and still benefit from
...

Loyal Merulina augment is the focus of this build because it allows you to have normal frame mobility while benefiting from damage reduction and status immunity (but not complete knockdown immunity). Loyal Merulina also has a unique effect where Merulina follows you around like a companion and automatically casts unique instances of Sea Snares. ## INTRODUCTION HELMINTH is optional between Nourish (to help sustain energy economy but doesn't really help Prism damage) or Pillage (helps survivability and Prism damage) Use prism, a disco ball of death, to shoot lasers to wipe out entire rooms rapidly. Each cast of 4 shoots 20 lasers which track enemies dealing 250 radiation damage tick twice a second, with each tick increasing damage of all lasers by 25% (or 50% with Solar Eclipse active). This means at around 200 strength and assuming a dense map where all 20 lasers constantly tick twice a second, after the first second each laser will deal 30,000 damage per tick.
